# Household hh_s9 — 42 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple_plus_one. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, entry, kitchen, living, office, storage.

Residents:

- **Ines** (resident_1): retired; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.61); average timing (jitter scale 1.17); rarely forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.29); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: friends, music, plants. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run.
- **Yuki** (resident_2): retired; very tidy (tidiness 0.94); average timing (jitter scale 1.04); often forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.36); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: meditation, movies, plants. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Hana** (resident_3): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.76); punctual (jitter scale 0.56); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.94); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the office desk. Hobbies: movies, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Yuki.

## Day 5 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d5`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:30–16:50).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d5`: Yuki gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:38–21:23).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_3:d5`: Hana has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `running_late:resident_2`: Yuki is running late all day (0.73): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.37, hurriedness 0.62, distraction 0.50; Yuki energy 0.98, hurriedness 0.73, distraction 0.44; Hana energy 0.46, hurriedness 0.55, distraction 0.39

## Day 12 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d12`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:36–16:29).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_3:d12`: Hana is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:17–16:47).
- `ep:overslept:resident_3:d12`: Hana overslept: a rushed morning with things dumped at the door, then a normal day (06:30–10:30).
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.11): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.73, hurriedness 0.58, distraction 0.68; Yuki energy 1.00, hurriedness 0.58, distraction 0.48; Hana energy 0.11, hurriedness 0.49, distraction 0.13

## Day 14 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d14`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:35–16:08).
- `ep:early_night:resident_1:d14`: Ines turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d14`: Ines gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:33–21:18).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_2:d14`: Yuki has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).

- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.07): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 18 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_3:d18`: Hana is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:04–16:12).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_3:d18`: Hana is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (11:01–13:01).

## Day 21 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d21`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:55–17:18).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d21`: Hana gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:02–20:47).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d21`: Ines is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (10:32–12:32).

## Day 22 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d22`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:46–15:16).
- `ep:early_night:resident_3:d22`: Hana turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_3:d22`: Hana is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:32–17:13).
- `ep:overslept:resident_3:d22`: Hana overslept: a rushed morning with things dumped at the door, then a normal day (06:30–10:30).

## Day 23 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d23`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:25–16:04).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d23`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:21–15:51).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_2:d23`: Yuki can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:13–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_3` (Hana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.01): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 25 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d25`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:48–16:42).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d25`: Yuki gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:12–19:57).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_3:d25`: Hana can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:51–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_3` (Hana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `distracted:resident_3`: Hana is distracted (0.82): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 28 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:early_night:resident_2:d28`: Yuki turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_3:d28`: Hana is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:11–17:00).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_2:d28`: Yuki can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:32–24:00).

## Day 29 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d29`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:24–16:21).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d29`: Ines is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:38–15:08).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.

## Day 31 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d31`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:00–16:22).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_2:d31`: Yuki can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (21:15–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_3` (Hana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `distracted:resident_1`: Ines is distracted (0.81): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.06): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.16, distraction 0.81; Yuki energy 0.06, hurriedness 0.17, distraction 0.41; Hana energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.15

## Day 32 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d32`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:15–17:17).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d32`: Ines is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:36–16:06).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d32`: Ines gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:00–19:45).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_3` (Hana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
